<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Calculate how much bigger is $3\dfrac{1}{4}$ is than $1\dfrac{3}{4}$ writing your answer as a decimal.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Calculate how much bigger is &#36;3\dfrac{1}{4}&#36; is than &#36;1\dfrac{3}{4}&#36; writing your answer as a decimal.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 3 \dfrac {1} {4} - 1 \dfrac {3} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {13} {4} - \dfrac {7} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {13 - 7} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {6} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 2} {2 \times 2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times \cancel {2}} {2 \times \cancel {2}} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3} {2} \\\\
&= 1.5
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$1.5$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">


<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;
\begin{aligned}
&= 3 \dfrac {1} {4} - 1 \dfrac {3} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {13} {4} - \dfrac {7} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {13 - 7} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {6} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 2} {2 \times 2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times \cancel {2}} {2 \times \cancel {2}} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3} {2} \\\\
&= 1.5
\end{aligned}
&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {1} {4} \text{ of } 48 \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {4} \times 48 \\\\
&= \dfrac {48} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {12 \times 4} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {12 \times \cancel{4}} {\cancel{4}} \\\\
&= 12
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Work out $\dfrac{1}{2}$ of $\dfrac{1}{3}$ of $\dfrac{1}{4}$ of $48$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Work out &#36;\dfrac{1}{2}&#36; of &#36;\dfrac{1}{3}&#36; of &#36;\dfrac{1}{4}&#36; of &#36;48&#36;.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {1} {2} \text{ of } \dfrac {1} {3} \text{ of } \dfrac {1} {4} \text{ of } 48 \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {2} \times \dfrac {1} {3} \times \dfrac {1} {4} \times 48 \\\\
&= \dfrac {48} {2 \times 3 \times 4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \times 3 \times 4 \times 2} {2 \times 3 \times 4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel{2} \times \cancel{3} \times \cancel{4} \times 2} {\cancel{2} \times \cancel{3} \times \cancel{4}} \\\\
&= 2
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$2$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">


<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {1} {2} \text{ of } \dfrac {1} {3} \text{ of } \dfrac {1} {4} \text{ of } 48 \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {2} \times \dfrac {1} {3} \times \dfrac {1} {4} \times 48 \\\\
&= \dfrac {48} {2 \times 3 \times 4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \times 3 \times 4 \times 2} {2 \times 3 \times 4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel{2} \times \cancel{3} \times \cancel{4} \times 2} {\cancel{2} \times \cancel{3} \times \cancel{4}} \\\\
&= 2
\end{aligned}
&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Write down the fraction of the school children who liked tennis, giving your answer in its lowest form.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Write down the fraction of the school children who liked tennis, giving your answer in its lowest form.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Tennis}                                   &= \dfrac {45} {360} \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {45} {45 \times 8} \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {\cancel {45}} {\cancel {45}  \times 8} \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {1} {8}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\dfrac {1} {8}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">


<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\text{Tennis}                                   &= \dfrac {45} {360} \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {45} {45 \times 8} \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {\cancel {45}} {\cancel {45}  \times 8} \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {1} {8}
\end{aligned}
&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Option Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Key Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A total of $240$ children were asked to complete the survey. Calculate how many of the children preferred cricket.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A total of &#36;240&#36; children were asked to complete the survey. Calculate how many of the children preferred cricket.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Cricket}                                  &= \dfrac {60} {360} \times 240 \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {60 \times 240} {360} \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {60 \times 6 \times 40} {60 \times 6} \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {\cancel{60} \times \cancel{6} \times 40} {\cancel{60} \times \cancel{6}} \\\\
                                                &= 40
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$40$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">


<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\text{Cricket}                                  &= \dfrac {60} {360} \times 240 \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {60 \times 240} {360} \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {60 \times 6 \times 40} {60 \times 6} \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {\cancel{60} \times \cancel{6} \times 40} {\cancel{60} \times \cancel{6}} \\\\
                                                &= 40
\end{aligned}
&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Option Raw</strong>

</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">

<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Answer Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;40&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Estimate how many children would say their favorite sport was football out of the whole school of $1200$ pupils.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Estimate how many children would say their favorite sport was football out of the whole school of &#36;1200&#36; pupils.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Football fraction}                        &= \dfrac {360 - 90 - 45 - 60 - 30} {360} \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {360 - 225} {360} \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {135} {360} \\\\
\text{Football children}                        &= \dfrac {135} {360} \times 1200 \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {135 \times 1200} {360} \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {3 \times 45 \times 120 \times 10} {120 \times 3} \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {\cancel 3 \times 45 \times \cancel {120} \times 10} {\cancel {120} \times \cancel 3} \\\\
                                                &= 45 \times 10 \\\\
                                                &= 450
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$450$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">


<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\text{Football fraction}                        &= \dfrac {360 - 90 - 45 - 60 - 30} {360} \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {360 - 225} {360} \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {135} {360} \\\\
\text{Football children}                        &= \dfrac {135} {360} \times 1200 \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {135 \times 1200} {360} \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {3 \times 45 \times 120 \times 10} {120 \times 3} \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {\cancel 3 \times 45 \times \cancel {120} \times 10} {\cancel {120} \times \cancel 3} \\\\
                                                &= 45 \times 10 \\\\
                                                &= 450
\end{aligned}
&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Answer Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;450&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Work out the mean (average) of the number of chocolate coins.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Work out the mean (average) of the number of chocolate coins.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">

ABBR: $\text{Mean} = \dfrac {\text{Total of items}} {\text{Number of items}}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Mean coins}                   &= \dfrac {8 + 7 + 11 + 6 + 9 + 7} {6} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {48} {6} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {6 \times 8} {6} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {\cancel{6} \times 8} {\cancel{6}} \\\\
                                    &= 8
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$8$

</div>
</div>

                                    &= \dfrac {48} {6} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {6 \times 8} {6} \\\\

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A multipack of $12$ cans of Ned's Cola costs $\pounds 6.95$. 

A single can of Ned's Cola costs $65 \ \text{pence}$ if bought individually. 

Calculate how much cheaper it is to buy a multipack of $12$ cans than to buy $12$ cans individually, giving your answer in pence.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A multipack of &#36;12&#36; cans of Ned's Cola costs &#36;\pounds 6.95&#36;. 

A single can of Ned's Cola costs &#36;65 \ \text{pence}&#36; if bought individually. 

Calculate how much cheaper it is to buy a multipack of &#36;12&#36; cans than to buy &#36;12&#36; cans individually, giving your answer in pence.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">

ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
_no_dot

</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Cost of 12 single cans}      &= 12 \times 65 \\\\
                                   &= 780 \ \text{p} \\\\
\text{Multipack cost}              &= \pounds 6.95 \\\\
                                   &= 695 \ \text{p} \\\\
\text{Cheaper by}                  &= 780 \ \text{p} - 695 \ \text{p} \\\\
                                   &= 85 \ \text{p}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$85 \ \text{p}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">rtq_abbr_note_md_unit_no_dot
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\text{Cost of 12 single cans}      &= 12 \times 65 \\\\
                                   &= 780 \ \text{p} \\\\
\text{Multipack cost}              &= \pounds 6.95 \\\\
                                   &= 695 \ \text{p} \\\\
\text{Cheaper by}                  &= 780 \ \text{p} - 695 \ \text{p} \\\\
                                   &= 85 \ \text{p}
\end{aligned}
&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Option Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Key Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Answer Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;85 \ \text{p}&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="topics" style="display: flex; justify-content: flex-start; margin: 0.85rem 0 1rem;">

<span style="display: inline-flex; align-items: center; border: 1px solid #9f1239; border-radius: 999px; background: #fce7f3; color: #881337; padding: 0.38rem 1rem; font-size: 1.2rem; font-weight: 800; letter-spacing: 0.02em;">
math.number.order
</span>
</li>
</ul>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Arrange the following fraction in numerical size order, starting with the smallest. 

$\dfrac{3}{10}, \quad \dfrac{1}{5}, \quad \dfrac{3}{2}, \quad \dfrac{1}{20}, \quad \dfrac{1}{4}$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Arrange the following fraction in numerical size order, starting with the smallest. 

&#36;\dfrac{3}{10}, \quad \dfrac{1}{5}, \quad \dfrac{3}{2}, \quad \dfrac{1}{20}, \quad \dfrac{1}{4}&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">

ABBR: Compare all values in one common form.

Preferred order:
- Decimals
- Percentages
- Same-denominator fractions

</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Compare</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

ABBR: Compare the values as fractions.


$\begin{aligned}
& \dfrac{3}{10} \quad \dfrac{1}{5} \quad \dfrac{3}{2} \quad \dfrac{1}{20} \quad \dfrac{1}{4} \\\\
& \dfrac{3 \times 2}{10 \times 2} \quad \dfrac{1 \times 4}{5 \times 4} \quad \dfrac{3 \times 10}{2 \times 10} \quad \dfrac{1}{20} \quad \dfrac{1 \times 5}{4 \times 5} \\\\
& \dfrac{6}{20} \quad \dfrac{4}{20} \quad \dfrac{30}{20} \quad \dfrac{1}{20} \quad \dfrac{5}{20}
\end{aligned}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

ABBR: In ascending order:


$\begin{aligned}
& \dfrac{1}{20} < \dfrac{4}{20} < \dfrac{5}{20} < \dfrac{6}{20} < \dfrac{30}{20} \\\\
& \dfrac{1}{20} < \dfrac{1}{5} < \dfrac{1}{4} < \dfrac{3}{10} < \dfrac{3}{2}
\end{aligned}$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\dfrac{1}{20} \quad \dfrac{1}{5} \quad \dfrac{1}{4} \quad \dfrac{3}{10} \quad \dfrac{3}{2}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">rtq_abbr_note_md_comparison_forms
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&lt;WorkingSection phase="setup" title="Compare"&gt;

rtq_abbr_working_note_md_torder_compare_as_fractions

&#36;\begin{aligned}
& \dfrac{3}{10} \quad \dfrac{1}{5} \quad \dfrac{3}{2} \quad \dfrac{1}{20} \quad \dfrac{1}{4} \\\\
& \dfrac{3 \times 2}{10 \times 2} \quad \dfrac{1 \times 4}{5 \times 4} \quad \dfrac{3 \times 10}{2 \times 10} \quad \dfrac{1}{20} \quad \dfrac{1 \times 5}{4 \times 5} \\\\
& \dfrac{6}{20} \quad \dfrac{4}{20} \quad \dfrac{30}{20} \quad \dfrac{1}{20} \quad \dfrac{5}{20}
\end{aligned}&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="conclusion" title="Answer"&gt;

rtq_abbr_working_note_md_torder_ascending

&#36;\begin{aligned}
& \dfrac{1}{20} &lt; \dfrac{4}{20} &lt; \dfrac{5}{20} &lt; \dfrac{6}{20} &lt; \dfrac{30}{20} \\\\
& \dfrac{1}{20} &lt; \dfrac{1}{5} &lt; \dfrac{1}{4} &lt; \dfrac{3}{10} &lt; \dfrac{3}{2}
\end{aligned}&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Option Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

An equilateral triangle is divided into smaller equilateral triangles as shown below. State how many angles of $60\degree$ there 
are in the diagram.

<div class="paper-image-layout" data-indent="none">
<img src="/assets/papers/dulwich-college/undated/specimen-paper-a/questions/s01-q13-s01-i00.png" alt="" data-slot="paper-image" data-asset-scope="question" data-alt-review="pending" data-kind="essential" data-display-size="sm" data-align="center" data-indent="none" class="paper-image" style="width: 100%; max-width: 16rem; margin: 1rem auto; height: auto; display: block;" />
</div>

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">An equilateral triangle is divided into smaller equilateral triangles as shown below. State how many angles of &#36;60\degree&#36; there 
are in the diagram.

&lt;PaperImage assetScope="question" kind="essential" /&gt;
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">

ABBR: $\text{Each angle of an equilateral triangle} = 60 \degree$

</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

The diagram has $9$ small equilateral triangles.

Each equilateral triangle has $3$ angles of $60\degree$.

Total number of $60\degree$ angles $= 9 \times 3 = 27$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$27$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">

<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">rtq_abbr_formula_katex_angles_of_equilateral_triangle
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The diagram has &#36;9&#36; small equilateral triangles.

Each equilateral triangle has &#36;3&#36; angles of &#36;60\degree&#36;.

Total number of &#36;60\degree&#36; angles &#36;= 9 \times 3 = 27&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Answer Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;27&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The diagram is now squashed to make the top angle $90\degree$, turning all the triangles into right-angled isosceles triangles. 
Calculate how many acute angles there are in the diagram now.

<div class="paper-image-layout" data-indent="none">
<img src="/assets/papers/dulwich-college/undated/specimen-paper-a/questions/s01-q13-s02-i00.png" alt="" data-slot="paper-image" data-asset-scope="question" data-alt-review="pending" data-kind="essential" data-display-size="sm" data-align="center" data-indent="none" class="paper-image" style="width: 100%; max-width: 16rem; margin: 1rem auto; height: auto; display: block;" />
</div>

</div>

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The diagram is now squashed to make the top angle &#36;90\degree&#36;, turning all the triangles into right-angled isosceles triangles. 
Calculate how many acute angles there are in the diagram now.

&lt;PaperImage assetScope="question" kind="essential" /&gt;
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

The diagram still has $9$ small triangles.

Each right-angled isosceles triangle has $2$ acute angles.

Total number of acute angles $= 9 \times 2 = 18$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$18$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

*ABBR: TODO:: Find the right katex symbol.*

Each shape in this grid is hiding a particular number so that the three shapes in any row or column add up to the value written 
at the end of that row or column. 


|  $\spadesuit$|   Sun        |   Sun       | $16$ |
|------------|------------|-----------|----|
| $\spadesuit$ | $\spadesuit$ | $\spadesuit$| $12$ |
| $\spadesuit$ | $\spadesuit$ | $\spadesuit$| $18$ |
| $\spadesuit$ | $14$         |   $?$       |  $+$ |


<div class="paper-image-layout" data-indent="none">
<img src="/assets/papers/dulwich-college/undated/specimen-paper-a/questions/s01-q15-i00.png" alt="" data-slot="paper-image" data-asset-scope="question" data-alt-review="pending" data-kind="essential" data-display-size="sm" data-align="center" data-indent="none" class="paper-image" style="width: 100%; max-width: 16rem; margin: 1rem auto; height: auto; display: block;" />
</div>

Work out the number that should replace the question mark.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">rtq_abbr_working_katex_show_correct_symbol
Each shape in this grid is hiding a particular number so that the three shapes in any row or column add up to the value written 
at the end of that row or column. 

&lt;PaperTable&gt;

|  &#36;\spadesuit&#36;|   Sun        |   Sun       | &#36;16&#36; |
|------------|------------|-----------|----|
| &#36;\spadesuit&#36; | &#36;\spadesuit&#36; | &#36;\spadesuit&#36;| &#36;12&#36; |
| &#36;\spadesuit&#36; | &#36;\spadesuit&#36; | &#36;\spadesuit&#36;| &#36;18&#36; |
| &#36;\spadesuit&#36; | &#36;14&#36;         |   &#36;?&#36;       |  &#36;+&#36; |

&lt;/PaperTable&gt;

</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Let the value of the diamond $= d$

Let the value of the sun $= s$

Let the value of the club $= c$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="equations" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Equations</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

From the question,


$
\begin{aligned}
d + 2s              &= 16 & (1) \\\\
3d                  &= 12 & (2) \\\\
2c + d              &= 18 & (3)
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Find the diamond</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Use equation $(2)$.

$
\begin{aligned}
3d                  &= 12 \\\\
d                   &= \dfrac {12} {3} \\\\
d                   &= \dfrac {3 \times 4} {3} \\\\
d                   &= \dfrac {\cancel{3} \times 4} {\cancel{3}} \\\\
d                   &= 4
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Find the sun</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Substitute $d = 4$ into equation $(1)$.

$
\begin{aligned}
d + 2s              &= 16 \\\\
4 + 2s              &= 16 \\\\
2s                  &= 16 - 4 \\\\
s                   &= \dfrac {12} {2} \\\\
s                   &= \dfrac {2 \times 6} {2} \\\\
s                   &= \dfrac {\cancel{2} \times 6} {\cancel{2}} \\\\
s                   &= 6
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Find the club</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Substitute $d = 4$ into equation $(3)$.

$
\begin{aligned}
2c + d              &= 18 \\\\
2c + 4              &= 18 \\\\
2c                  &= 18 - 4 \\\\
c                   &= \dfrac {14} {2} \\\\
c                   &= \dfrac {2 \times 7} {2} \\\\
c                   &= \dfrac {\cancel{2} \times 7} {\cancel{2}} \\\\
c                   &= 7
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Missing value}            &= s + d + c \\\\
                                &= 6 + 4 + 7 \\\\
                                &= 17
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$17$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">


<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&lt;WorkingSection phase="setup"&gt;

Let the value of the diamond &#36;= d&#36;

Let the value of the sun &#36;= s&#36;

Let the value of the club &#36;= c&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="equations" title="Equations"&gt;

rtq_abbr_working_md_opener_question

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
d + 2s              &= 16 & (1) \\\\
3d                  &= 12 & (2) \\\\
2c + d              &= 18 & (3)
\end{aligned}
&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="solve" title="Find the diamond"&gt;

Use equation &#36;(2)&#36;.

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
3d                  &= 12 \\\\
d                   &= \dfrac {12} {3} \\\\
d                   &= \dfrac {3 \times 4} {3} \\\\
d                   &= \dfrac {\cancel{3} \times 4} {\cancel{3}} \\\\
d                   &= 4
\end{aligned}
&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="solve" title="Find the sun"&gt;

Substitute &#36;d = 4&#36; into equation &#36;(1)&#36;.

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
d + 2s              &= 16 \\\\
4 + 2s              &= 16 \\\\
2s                  &= 16 - 4 \\\\
s                   &= \dfrac {12} {2} \\\\
s                   &= \dfrac {2 \times 6} {2} \\\\
s                   &= \dfrac {\cancel{2} \times 6} {\cancel{2}} \\\\
s                   &= 6
\end{aligned}
&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="solve" title="Find the club"&gt;

Substitute &#36;d = 4&#36; into equation &#36;(3)&#36;.

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
2c + d              &= 18 \\\\
2c + 4              &= 18 \\\\
2c                  &= 18 - 4 \\\\
c                   &= \dfrac {14} {2} \\\\
c                   &= \dfrac {2 \times 7} {2} \\\\
c                   &= \dfrac {\cancel{2} \times 7} {\cancel{2}} \\\\
c                   &= 7
\end{aligned}
&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="conclusion" title="Answer"&gt;

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\text{Missing value}            &= s + d + c \\\\
                                &= 6 + 4 + 7 \\\\
                                &= 17
\end{aligned}
&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Rhys and Sarah are on their bicycles and start $90 \ \text{miles}$ apart on a road, riding towards each other. 
Rhys is travelling at $10 \ \text{mph}$ and Sarah is travelling at $20 \ \text{mph}$. They set off at the same time and both keep moving at constant speeds until they meet.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Rhys and Sarah are on their bicycles and start &#36;90 \ \text{miles}&#36; apart on a road, riding towards each other. 
Rhys is travelling at &#36;10 \ \text{mph}&#36; and Sarah is travelling at &#36;20 \ \text{mph}&#36;. They set off at the same time and both keep moving at constant speeds until they meet.
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Work out at what distance each rider has cycled by the time they meet.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Work out at what distance each rider has cycled by the time they meet.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">

ABBR: $\text {Distance} = \text{Speed} \times \text{Time}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Let the time taken to meet $= t$ hours

When they meet, the total distance travelled is $90 \ \text{miles}$.

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Equation</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

From the question,


$
\begin{aligned}
10 \times t + 20 \times t &= 90 \\\\
10t + 20t                 &= 90 \\\\
30t                       &= 90 \\\\
t                         &= \dfrac{90}{30} \\\\
t                         &= \dfrac{3 \times 30}{30} \\\\
t                         &= \dfrac{3 \times \cancel{30}}{\cancel{30}} \\\\
t                         &= 3 \text{ hours}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Distances</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Distance travelled by Rhys}  &= 10 \times 3 \\\\
                                   &= 30 \text{ miles} \\\\
\text{Distance travelled by Sarah} &= 20 \times 3 \\\\
                                   &= 60 \text{ miles}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Rhys cycled $30 \ \text{miles}$ and Sarah cycled $60 \ \text{miles}$.

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">

ABBR: Alternate solution

</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Whole to part ratio</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Using whole to part ratio,

$
\begin{array}{cccccccccccccc}
  & \text{total distance}     &:& \text{Rhys}          &:& \text{Sarah} \\\\
= & (10 + 20)                 &:& 10                   &:& 20 \\\\
= & 30                        &:& 10                   &:& 20 \\\\
= & 30 \times 3               &:& 10 \times 3          &:& 20 \times 3 \\\\
= & 90 \ \text{miles}         &:& 30 \ \text{miles}    &:& 60 \ \text{miles}
\end{array}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Rhys cycled $30 \ \text{miles}$ and Sarah cycled $60 \ \text{miles}$.

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$30 \text{ miles}$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$60 \text{ miles}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">

<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Write down the time taken for them to meet.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Find the area of rectangle shown below.

<div class="paper-image-layout" data-indent="none">
<img src="/assets/papers/dulwich-college/undated/specimen-paper-a/questions/s01-q17-s01-i00.png" alt="" data-slot="paper-image" data-asset-scope="question" data-alt-review="pending" data-kind="essential" data-display-size="sm" data-align="center" data-indent="none" class="paper-image" style="width: 100%; max-width: 16rem; margin: 1rem auto; height: auto; display: block;" />
</div>

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Find the area of rectangle shown below.

&lt;PaperImage assetScope="question" kind="essential" /&gt;
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

$
\begin{aligned}

            &= 1200 \ \text{mm}^2
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

<div class="topics" style="display: flex; justify-content: flex-start; margin: 0.85rem 0 1rem;">

inherits parent tags: yes
</span>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

George cuts the rectangle up into an exact number of right-angled triangles, each with sides as shown in the diagram below. 

<div class="paper-image-layout" data-indent="none">
<img src="/assets/papers/dulwich-college/undated/specimen-paper-a/questions/s01-q17-s02-i00.png" alt="" data-slot="paper-image" data-asset-scope="question" data-alt-review="pending" data-kind="essential" data-display-size="sm" data-align="center" data-indent="none" class="paper-image" style="width: 100%; max-width: 16rem; margin: 1rem auto; height: auto; display: block;" />
</div>

Calculate the number of triangles that he cuts from the rectangle.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">George cuts the rectangle up into an exact number of right-angled triangles, each with sides as shown in the diagram below. 

&lt;PaperImage assetScope="question" kind="essential" /&gt;

Calculate the number of triangles that he cuts from the rectangle.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">

ABBR: $\text{Area of triangle} = \dfrac{1}{2} \times \text{base} \times \text{height}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Area of one triangle</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Area of triangle}         &= \dfrac {1} {2} \times 12 \times 5 \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {1} {2} \times 60 \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {60} {2} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {30 \times 2} {2} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {30 \times \cancel{2}} {\cancel{2}} \\\\
                                &= 30 \ \text{mm}^2
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Number of triangles</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of triangles}                      &= \dfrac {\text{Area of rectangle}} {\text{Area of triangle}} \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {1200} {30} \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {40 \times 30} {30} \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {40 \times \cancel{30}} {\cancel{30}} \\\\
                                                &= 40
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$40$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">

<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">rtq_abbr_formula_katex_area_triangle_expanded
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&lt;WorkingSection phase="solve" title="Area of one triangle"&gt;

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\text{Area of triangle}         &= \dfrac {1} {2} \times 12 \times 5 \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {1} {2} \times 60 \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {60} {2} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {30 \times 2} {2} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {30 \times \cancel{2}} {\cancel{2}} \\\\
                                &= 30 \ \text{mm}^2
\end{aligned}
&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="solve" title="Number of triangles"&gt;

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of triangles}                      &= \dfrac {\text{Area of rectangle}} {\text{Area of triangle}} \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {1200} {30} \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {40 \times 30} {30} \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {40 \times \cancel{30}} {\cancel{30}} \\\\
                                                &= 40
\end{aligned}
&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Convert this distance from millimetres into meters.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Convert this distance from millimetres into meters.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">

ABBR: $1 \ \text{m} = 1000 \ \text{mm}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 1200 \ \text{mm}   \\\\                      
&= \dfrac {1200} {1000} \ \text{m} \\\\
&= \dfrac {12\cancel{0}\cancel{0}} {10\cancel{0}\cancel{0}} \ \text{m} \\\\
&= \dfrac {12} {10} \ \text{m} \\\\
&= 1.2 \ \text{m}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

$1.2 \ \text{m}$

</div>
</div>

</div>

&= 1200 \ \text{mm}   \\\\

&= \dfrac {12\cancel{0}\cancel{0}} {10\cancel{0}\cancel{0}} \ \text{m} \\\\

\end{aligned}

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

On the island of Pythageuleria all vehicle number plates have $2$ letters from the selection $\text {A, B, C, D}$ followed by a number 
$1, 2, 3$ or $4$. 

For example:

$\boxed{ \text {A D 1} } \qquad 
\boxed{ \text {A D 4} } \qquad 
\boxed{ \text {C A 3} } \qquad 
\boxed{ \text {D D 2} }$

Note that repeated letters are allowed.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">On the island of Pythageuleria all vehicle number plates have &#36;2&#36; letters from the selection &#36;\text {A, B, C, D}&#36; followed by a number 
&#36;1, 2, 3&#36; or &#36;4&#36;. 

For example:

&#36;\boxed{ \text {A D 1} } \qquad 
\boxed{ \text {A D 4} } \qquad 
\boxed{ \text {C A 3} } \qquad 
\boxed{ \text {D D 2} }&#36;

Note that repeated letters are allowed.
</code></pre>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Calculate how many plates contain just one vowel and an odd number.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</code></pre>

<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

The only vowel is $\text{A}$, so exactly one of the two letters must be $\text{A}$.

The odd numbers are $1$ and $3$.

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="verify" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">A in the first place</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

If we use $\text{A}$ in the first place, the possible arrangements are:

- $\text{AB1}$
- $\text{AB3}$
- $\text{AC1}$
- $\text{AC3}$
- $\text{AD1}$
- $\text{AD3}$

Count $= 6$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="verify" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">A in the second place</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

If we use $\text{A}$ in the second place, the possible arrangements are:

- $\text{BA1}$
- $\text{BA3}$
- $\text{CA1}$
- $\text{CA3}$
- $\text{DA1}$
- $\text{DA3}$

Count $= 6$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Count from the list</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total plates} &= 6 + 6 \\\\
                    &= 12
\end{aligned}
$

</div>

$

\text{Odd number on plate}        &= 2 \text{ choices} \\\\
\text{Plates}                     &= 1 \times 3 \times 2 \\\\
                                  &= 6

<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{First letter}               &= 3 \text{ choices} \\\\
\text{Second letter }(\text{A})   &= 1 \text{ choice} \\\\
\text{Odd number on plate}        &= 2 \text{ choices} \\\\
\text{Plates}                     &= 3 \times 1 \times 2 \\\\
                                  &= 6
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">

<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total plates} &= 6 + 6 \\\\
                    &= 12
\end{aligned}
$

</div>

<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion" id="question-dulwich-college-11-plus-maths-undated-specimen-paper-a-1-18-4">
<div class="uuid">
<p>0F252D9E-6080-4319-B838-77FA8DF2AF83</p>
</div>
<div class="topics" style="display: flex; justify-content: flex-start; margin: 0.85rem 0 1rem;">
<ul style="display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; justify-content: flex-start; gap: 0.55rem; margin: 0; padding: 0; list-style: none;">
<li style="margin: 0; padding: 0;">
<span style="display: inline-flex; align-items: center; border: 1px solid #9f1239; border-radius: 999px; background: #fce7f3; color: #881337; padding: 0.38rem 1rem; font-size: 1.2rem; font-weight: 800; letter-spacing: 0.02em;">
math.permutation
</span>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div style="display: flex; justify-content: flex-start; margin: -0.4rem 0 1rem;">
<span style="display: inline-flex; align-items: center; border: 1px solid #15803d; border-radius: 999px; background: #dcfce7; color: #14532d; padding: 0.24rem 0.7rem; font-size: 0.95rem; font-weight: 700;">
inherits parent tags: yes
</span>
</div>
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Given that there are $64$ possible codes in total, calculate what fraction of the number plates have their own two letters in 
alphabetical order, giving your answer in its lowest form.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Given that there are &#36;64&#36; possible codes in total, calculate what fraction of the number plates have their own two letters in 
alphabetical order, giving your answer in its lowest form.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Alphabetical order means the first letter must come before the second letter, or be the same as the second letter.

For example, $\text{AB}$ is valid, but $\text{BA}$ is not valid.

Repeated letters are allowed, so $\text{AA}$, $\text{BB}$, $\text{CC}$ and $\text{DD}$ are valid.

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="verify" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Starts with A</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

If we use $\text{A}$ in the first place, the possible letter pairs are:

$
\begin{matrix}
\text{AA} & \text{AB} & \text{AC} & \text{AD}
\end{matrix}
$

Count $= 4$ letter pairs

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="verify" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Starts with B</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

If we use $\text{B}$ in the first place, the possible letter pairs are:

$
\begin{matrix}
\text{BB} & \text{BC} & \text{BD}
\end{matrix}
$

Count $= 3$ letter pairs

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="verify" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Starts with C</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

If we use $\text{C}$ in the first place, the possible letter pairs are:

$
\begin{matrix}
\text{CC} & \text{CD}
\end{matrix}
$

Count $= 2$ letter pairs

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="verify" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Starts with D</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

If we use $\text{D}$ in the first place, the possible letter pair is:

$
\begin{matrix}
\text{DD}
\end{matrix}
$

Count $= 1$ letter pair

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Count the plates</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Alphabetical letter pairs} &= 4 + 3 + 2 + 1 \\\\
                                 &= 10 \\\\
\text{Number plates}             &= 10 \times 4 \\\\
                                 &= 40 \\\\
\text{Fraction}                  &= \dfrac {40} {64} \\\\
                                 &= \dfrac {8 \times 5} {8 \times 8} \\\\
                                 &= \dfrac {\cancel{8} \times 5} {\cancel{8} \times 8} \\\\
                                 &= \dfrac {5} {8}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\dfrac {5} {8}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">


<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&lt;WorkingSection phase="setup"&gt;

Alphabetical order means the first letter must come before the second letter, or be the same as the second letter.

For example, &#36;\text{AB}&#36; is valid, but &#36;\text{BA}&#36; is not valid.

Repeated letters are allowed, so &#36;\text{AA}&#36;, &#36;\text{BB}&#36;, &#36;\text{CC}&#36; and &#36;\text{DD}&#36; are valid.

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="verify" title="Starts with A"&gt;

If we use &#36;\text{A}&#36; in the first place, the possible letter pairs are:

&#36;
\begin{matrix}

&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="solve" title="Count the plates"&gt;

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\text{Alphabetical letter pairs} &= 4 + 3 + 2 + 1 \\\\
                                 &= 10 \\\\
\text{Number plates}             &= 10 \times 4 \\\\
                                 &= 40 \\\\
\text{Fraction}                  &= \dfrac {40} {64} \\\\
                                 &= \dfrac {8 \times 5} {8 \times 8} \\\\
                                 &= \dfrac {\cancel{8} \times 5} {\cancel{8} \times 8} \\\\

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

In four years time, Sam will be twice as old as he was four years ago. Calculate Sam's current age.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">In four years time, Sam will be twice as old as he was four years ago. Calculate Sam's current age.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Let Sam's current age $= a$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Equation</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

From the question,


$
\begin{aligned}
a + 4     &= 2(a - 4) \\\\
a + 4     &= 2a - 8 \\\\
2a - a    &= 8 + 4 \\\\
a         &= 12 \ \text{years}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">

<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Sam's current age $=12 \ \text{years}$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$12 \ \text{years}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">


<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="solve" title="Equation"&gt;

\end{aligned}
&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

I plant some seeds and get three white flowers. Write down how many blue flowers I should expect to grow.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">I plant some seeds and get three white flowers. Write down how many blue flowers I should expect to grow.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Using ratios,

$
\begin{array}{cccccccccccccc}
  & \text{white}                  &:& \text{blue} \\\\
= & 1                             &:& 3 \\\\
= & 1 \times 3                    &:& 3 \times 3 \\\\
= & 3                             &:& 9
\end{array}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Expected number of blue flowers $= 9$

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">

ABBR: Alternate solution

</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Let the expected number of blue flowers $= b$.

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Solve the ratio</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Using equivalent ratio,

$
\begin{aligned}
1 \ \text{white} : 3 \ \text{blue}              &= 3 \ \text{white} : b \ \text{blue} \\\\
\dfrac{1} {3}                                   &= \dfrac {3} {b} \\\\
1 \times b                                      &= 3 \times 3 \\\\
b                                               &= 9
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
