<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Expected number of blue flowers $= 9$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$9$

</div>
</div>

<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion" id="question-dulwich-college-11-plus-maths-undated-specimen-paper-a-1-21-2">

<div class="topics" style="display: flex; justify-content: flex-start; margin: 0.85rem 0 1rem;">

<div style="display: flex; justify-content: flex-start; margin: -0.4rem 0 1rem;">

inherits parent tags: yes
</span>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

I plant a total of $30$ seeds in another patch of the garden. Calculate how many white flowers I would expect to grow in this patch.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">I plant a total of &#36;30&#36; seeds in another patch of the garden. Calculate how many white flowers I would expect to grow in this patch.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Using whole to part ratio,

$
\begin{array}{cccccccccccccc}
  & \text{flowers}                                    &:& \text{white} \\\\
= & 1 + 2 + 3                                         &:& 1 \\\\
= & 6                                                 &:& 1 \\\\
= & 6 \times 5                                        &:& 1 \times 5 \\\\
= & 30                                                &:& 5
\end{array}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Expected number of white flowers $= 5$

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">

ABBR: Alternate solution

</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Let the expected number of white flowers $= a$.

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Solve the ratio</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Using equivalent ratio,

$
\begin{aligned}
6 \ \text{flowers} : 1 \ \text{white}              &= 30 \ \text{flowers} : a \ \text{white} \\\\
\dfrac {6} {1}                                     &= \dfrac {30} {a} \\\\
6 \times a                                         &= 1 \times 30 \\\\
a                                                  &= \dfrac {30} {6} \\\\
a                                                  &= \dfrac {6 \times 5} {6} \\\\
a                                                  &= \dfrac {\cancel 6 \times 5} {\cancel 6} \\\\
a                                                  &= 5
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Expected number of white flowers $= 5$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$5$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">


<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&lt;WorkingSection phase="solve"&gt;

Using whole to part ratio,

&#36;
\begin{array}{cccccccccccccc}
  & \text{flowers}                                    &:& \text{white} \\\\
= & 1 + 2 + 3                                         &:& 1 \\\\
= & 6                                                 &:& 1 \\\\
= & 6 \times 5                                        &:& 1 \times 5 \\\\
= & 30                                                &:& 5
\end{array}
&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="conclusion" title="Answer"&gt;

Expected number of white flowers &#36;= 5&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">

<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&lt;WorkingSection phase="setup"&gt;

Let the expected number of white flowers &#36;= a&#36;.

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="solve" title="Solve the ratio"&gt;

Using equivalent ratio,

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
6 \ \text{flowers} : 1 \ \text{white}              &= 30 \ \text{flowers} : a \ \text{white} \\\\
\dfrac {6} {1}                                     &= \dfrac {30} {a} \\\\
6 \times a                                         &= 1 \times 30 \\\\
a                                                  &= \dfrac {30} {6} \\\\
a                                                  &= \dfrac {6 \times 5} {6} \\\\
a                                                  &= \dfrac {\cancel 6 \times 5} {\cancel 6} \\\\
a                                                  &= 5
\end{aligned}
&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="conclusion" title="Answer"&gt;

Expected number of white flowers &#36;= 5&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

In another area of the garden, $24$ blue flowers are grown. Calculate how many pink flowers I should expect to find here.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">In another area of the garden, &#36;24&#36; blue flowers are grown. Calculate how many pink flowers I should expect to find here.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Using ratios,

$
\begin{array}{cccccccccccccc}
  & \text{blue}                               &:& \text{pink} \\\\
= & 3                                         &:& 2 \\\\
= & 3 \times 8                                &:& 2 \times 8 \\\\
= & 24                                        &:& 16
\end{array}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Expected number of pink flowers $= 16$

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">

ABBR: Alternate solution

</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Let the expected number of pink flowers $= p$.

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Solve the ratio</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Using equivalent ratio,

$
\begin{aligned}
3 \ \text{blue} : 2 \ \text{pink}               &= 24 \ \text{blue} : p \ \text{pink} \\\\
\dfrac{3}{2}                                    &= \dfrac {24} {p} \\\\
3 \times p                                      &= 24 \times 2 \\\\
p                                               &= \dfrac {24 \times 2} {3} \\\\
p                                               &= \dfrac {8 \times 3 \times 2} {3} \\\\
p                                               &= \dfrac {8 \times \cancel 3 \times 2} {\cancel 3} \\\\
p                                               &= 8 \times 2 \\\\
p                                               &= 16
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">

<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Expected number of pink flowers $= 16$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$16$

**Question**

A packet of different type of seed produces red, orange or yellow flowers. Some students in a biology class plant $150$ seeds. 
All $150$ grow to produce flowers and the students draw up the results of the flower colours into a table and a bar chart as below.

|------------------|---|------|--------|
| Number of flowsers |     |        |   $72$   |

**Tips**

</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">

ABBR: $\text{Probability}  = \dfrac { \text{ Favourable outcomes} } { \text{Total outcomes} }$

</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Find probabilities</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{P(red or orange flower)}    &= \dfrac {20 + 58} {150} \\\\
                                  &= \dfrac {78} {150} \\\\
\text{P(purple flower)}           &= \dfrac {0} {150} \\\\
\text{P(orange or yellow flower)} &= \dfrac {58 + 72} {150} \\\\
                                  &= \dfrac {130} {150} \\\\
\text{P(yellow flower)}           &= \dfrac {72} {150}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Order of likelihood, from least likely to most likely:

-  picking a purple flower
-  picking a yellow flower
-  picking a red or orange flower
-  picking an orange or yellow flower

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$2, 4, 1, 3$

</div>

<div class="rag">

</div>
<div class="rag">
<p>rag_wf_comingsoon</p>
</div>
<div class="rag">
<p>rag_wf_prns</p>
</div>
<div class="topics" style="display: flex; justify-content: flex-start; margin: 0.85rem 0 1rem;">
<ul style="display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; justify-content: flex-start; gap: 0.55rem; margin: 0; padding: 0; list-style: none;">
<li style="margin: 0; padding: 0;">
<span style="display: inline-flex; align-items: center; border: 1px solid #9f1239; border-radius: 999px; background: #fce7f3; color: #881337; padding: 0.38rem 1rem; font-size: 1.2rem; font-weight: 800; letter-spacing: 0.02em;">
math.number.fraction
</span>
</li>
<li style="margin: 0; padding: 0;">
<span style="display: inline-flex; align-items: center; border: 1px solid #9f1239; border-radius: 999px; background: #fce7f3; color: #881337; padding: 0.38rem 1rem; font-size: 1.2rem; font-weight: 800; letter-spacing: 0.02em;">
frame.algebra
</span>
</li>
</ul>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The diagram below is made from three squares.

<div class="paper-image-layout" data-indent="none">
<img src="/assets/papers/dulwich-college/undated/specimen-paper-a/questions/s01-q23-i00.png" alt="" data-slot="paper-image" data-asset-scope="question" data-alt-review="pending" data-kind="essential" data-display-size="sm" data-align="center" data-indent="none" class="paper-image" style="width: 100%; max-width: 16rem; margin: 1rem auto; height: auto; display: block;" />
</div>

Calculate the fraction of the larger square that is shaded, giving your answer in its lowest form.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The diagram below is made from three squares.

&lt;PaperImage assetScope="question" kind="essential" /&gt;

Calculate the fraction of the larger square that is shaded, giving your answer in its lowest form.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">

ABBR: $\text{Area of square} = \text{side} \times \text{side}$

</div>
<div class="formula">

ABBR: $\text{Diagonal of square} = \sqrt{2} \times \text{side}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

<div class="paper-image-layout" data-indent="none">
<img src="/assets/papers/missing/missing_image.svg" alt="" data-slot="paper-image" data-asset-scope="working" data-alt-review="pending" data-kind="essential" data-display-size="sm" data-align="center" data-indent="none" class="paper-image" style="width: 100%; max-width: 16rem; margin: 1rem auto; height: auto; display: block;" />
</div>

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Side lengths</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">


Let the side length of the innermost square $= s$

The side of the middle square is the same as the diagonal of the inner square.

The side of the outer square is the same as the diagonal of the middle square.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Inner square side}     &= s \\\\
\text{Middle square side}    &= \sqrt{2} \times s \\\\
                             &= \sqrt{2}s \\\\
\text{Outer square side}     &= \sqrt{2} \times \sqrt{2}s \\\\
                             &= 2s
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Areas</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Outer square area}     &= 2s \times 2s \\\\
                             &= 4s^2 \\\\
\text{Middle square area}    &= \sqrt{2}s \times \sqrt{2}s \\\\
                             &= 2s^2 \\\\
\text{Inner square area}     &= s \times s \\\\
                             &= s^2 \\\\
\text{Shaded area}           &= 2s^2 - s^2 \\\\
                             &= s^2
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Shaded fraction</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Shaded fraction} &= \dfrac {\text{Shaded area}} {\text{Outer square area}} \\\\
                       &= \dfrac {s^2} {4s^2} \\\\
                       &= \dfrac {1} {4}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\dfrac {1} {4}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">

<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">rtq_abbr_formula_katex_area_square_expanded
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">rtq_abbr_formula_katex_diagonal_square_expanded
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&lt;WorkingSection phase="setup" title=""&gt;

&lt;PaperImage assetScope="working" kind="essential" /&gt;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="setup" title="Side lengths"&gt;


Let the side length of the innermost square &#36;= s&#36;

The side of the middle square is the same as the diagonal of the inner square.

The side of the outer square is the same as the diagonal of the middle square.

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\text{Inner square side}     &= s \\\\
\text{Middle square side}    &= \sqrt{2} \times s \\\\
                             &= \sqrt{2}s \\\\
\text{Outer square side}     &= \sqrt{2} \times \sqrt{2}s \\\\
                             &= 2s
\end{aligned}
&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="solve" title="Areas"&gt;

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\text{Outer square area}     &= 2s \times 2s \\\\
                             &= 4s^2 \\\\
\text{Middle square area}    &= \sqrt{2}s \times \sqrt{2}s \\\\
                             &= 2s^2 \\\\
\text{Inner square area}     &= s \times s \\\\
                             &= s^2 \\\\
\text{Shaded area}           &= 2s^2 - s^2 \\\\
                             &= s^2
\end{aligned}
&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="solve" title="Shaded fraction"&gt;

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\text{Shaded fraction} &= \dfrac {\text{Shaded area}} {\text{Outer square area}} \\\\
                       &= \dfrac {s^2} {4s^2} \\\\
                       &= \dfrac {1} {4}
\end{aligned}
&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Option Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">

<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
